# Direction on predicted movers (B25) - run run1

tickers sampled 2600 (seed 7), pool 100, picks [30, 10, 100], first test year 2018. Survivor-only price panel: every accuracy below is optimistic if anything (delisted names are missing).

## pick30_up_first

18,360 picks over 612 weeks; realised-mover rate among picks 0.726; base rate of `up_first` = 0.483 (always-up scores 0.517); test years 2018-2026, 10,743 scored rows per model.
